Conclusion: 量子Ising临界性的普适标度结构延伸到邻近的禁闭区域，统一了介子谱的组织方式。这一发现为在混合场中解释准一维Ising类磁体（如Ba(Sr)Co₂V₂O₈）的激发谱提供了超越E₈可积性的实用判据。

Abstract: Near $(1+1)$-dimensional [$(1+1)$D] Ising criticality, a magnetic perturbation induces confinement and produces a cascade of bound-state excitations known as mesons. Here we show these mesons share a universal mass scaling after independently rescaling the model-dependent microscopic couplings. The number of stable mesons is controlled by the lightest two-meson threshold, while the lightest-meson mass follows a continuous trajectory characterized by a single scaling parameter. Using Hamiltonian truncation method, we obtain the trajectory numerically in both Ising field theory and the near-critical mixed-field Ising chain (MFIC). Under the rescaling, the trajectory and stable-meson-count crossover windows of MFIC both collapse onto the field-theory results. To further demonstrate the above universal organization of the meson spectra, we consider a class of four-periodic spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg-Ising chains under transverse fields, whose parameter space contains a family of quantum Ising critical points. The Hamiltonian family includes effective spin models for the quasi-one-dimensional antiferromagnets Ba(Sr)Co$_2$V$_2$O$_8$. Using tensor-network calculations, we obtain the corresponding lightest-meson mass trajectory for BaCo$_2$V$_2$O$_8$ and find that it also collapse onto the same universal curve given by field-theory result. Our results suggest that the universal scaling structure of quantum Ising criticality extends into the nearby confining regime, governing the organization of the meson spectrum. They thereby provide a practical criterion for interpreting excitations of quasi-1D Ising-like magnets in mixed fields beyond $E_8$ integrability.

</details>


### [58] [Nonlinear Drude weight of the one-dimensional Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20269)
*Tetsuya Iwasaki,Hosho Katsura*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文结合精确Bethe-ansatz计算与低能有效场论，研究了1D排斥Hubbard模型中非线性Drude权（NLDWs）的性质。在四分之一填充下，推导了强耦合展开并与数值结果对比；在Tomonaga-Luttinger液体描述中，Umklapp相互作用预测超过某一阈值阶数的NLDWs发散，但精确Bethe-ansatz的有限尺寸标度表明所有NLDWs在热力学极限下有限，二者存在矛盾。在半填充下，分析了Mott金属-绝缘体转变附近NLDWs的有限尺寸标度、绝缘相渐近行为及临界点处的hyperscaling猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性输运系数（尤其是高阶Drude权）在强关联一维系统中的行为尚不清楚。传统上，低能有效场论（如TLL描述）是研究一维系统输运性质的主要工具，但其对更高阶非线性输运的预测是否可靠缺乏系统验证。本文旨在通过精确方法（Bethe-ansatz）与有效场论的系统对比，澄清两者在预测NLDWs时的差异及其物理来源，从而评估低能有效描述的适用范围与局限。

Method: 结合两种方法：一是精确Bethe-ansatz求解1D排斥Hubbard模型，通过有限尺寸标度提取热力学极限下的NLDWs；二是基于Tomonaga-Luttinger液体（TLL）低能有效场论，包含与带曲率和Umklapp散射相关的不可约微扰修正。在四分之一填充下，先对NLDWs做强耦合展开并与数值结果交叉验证；随后对比数值结果与TLL预言。在半填充下，分析NLDWs在Mott金属-绝缘体转变附近的有限尺寸标度行为，并推导绝缘相中的渐近形式，提出临界点附近的hyperscaling假设并通过数值验证。

Result: 在四分之一填充下，带曲率修正给出高阶NLDWs的有限贡献，而Umklapp相互作用预言当Drude权阶数n超过由TLL参数决定的门槛时NLDWs发散。但精确Bethe-ansatz的有限尺寸标度显示所有计算得到的NLDWs在热力学极限下均保持有限，与低能有效场论预言不一致。在半填充下，获得了NLDWs在Mott转变附近的行为，推导了绝缘相的渐近展开，并提出了临界点附近的hyperscaling ansatz，且该ansatz得到了数值验证。

Conclusion: 本文系统阐明了1D Hubbard模型中非线性输运系数（NLDWs）的相互作用依赖性和临界标度行为，揭示了低能有效场论对高阶（非线性）输运预测的固有局限。特别是在四分之一填充下，TLL框架中Umklapp散射导致的NLDWs发散预言与精确结果矛盾，表明常规低能有效描述不适用于更高阶输运系数的定量预测。这一发现对理解强关联一维系统的非线性响应具有重要意义，也提示需要发展超越现有有效场论的框架来处理高阶输运问题。

Abstract: We investigate nonlinear Drude weights (NLDWs) in the one-dimensional repulsive Hubbard model by combining exact Bethe-ansatz calculations with low-energy effective field theory. At quarter filling, we first derive the strong-coupling expansion of the NLDWs and confirm it numerically over a wide range of interaction strength. We then compare the numerical results with the prediction of the Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid (TLL) description including irrelevant perturbations. While band-curvature corrections yield finite contributions to higher-order NLDWs, the Umklapp interaction predicts divergent NLDWs when the order $n$ of the Drude weight exceeds a threshold determined by the TLL parameter. In contrast, finite-size scaling of the exact Bethe-ansatz results indicates that all calculated NLDWs remain finite in the thermodynamic limit, revealing a discrepancy between the exact results and the predictions of the low-energy effective field theory. At half filling, we analyze the finite-size scaling of the NLDWs across the Mott metal-insulator transition. We derive their asymptotic behavior in the insulating phase and propose a hyperscaling ansatz for NLDWs near the critical point, which is verified numerically. Our results clarify the interaction dependence and critical scaling of nonlinear transport coefficients in the one-dimensional Hubbard model and highlight limitations of the conventional low-energy effective description for higher-order transport.

</details>

### [59] [Dummy RAPM:Representing Low-Minute Players in Regularized Adjusted Plus-Minus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19454)
*Kenny Watts,Jonathan Pipping-Gamón,Abraham J. Wyner*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出Dummy RAPM方法，通过为每个阵容中排除的低分钟球员数量添加五个指示变量，恢复常规RAPM移除低分钟球员后丢失的阵容完整性信息。在16个NBA赛季的验证中，该方法以10分钟/出现次数为阈值和2.2的哑变量与球员惩罚比，在3-4月留出集上将赛季比赛得分差RMSE从12.897降至12.856，并在13/16个赛季表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规RAPM在移除低分钟球员列后，设计矩阵无法再表示完整的阵容信息，导致球员贡献估计存在信息损失。作者希望恢复这部分信息以提升预测准确性。

Method: 提出Dummy RAPM方法：在每个阵容两侧添加五个指示变量，分别表示被排除球员的数量（0-4或更多），从而保留阵容完整性的信息。通过16个NBA赛季的时间顺序验证（chronological validation）确定最优参数：10分钟/出现次数的阈值和2.2的哑变量与球员惩罚比。

Result: 在留出的3-4月比赛中，Dummy RAPM将平均赛季比赛得分差RMSE从12.897降至12.856，在16个赛季中有13个赛季取得更低RMSE，平均降低0.042分（0.30%）。

Conclusion: 虽然比赛层面的预测准确性提升幅度很小，但改进是一致且稳健的：当RAPM记录每侧被排除球员的数量时，其表现会更好。

Abstract: Regularized Adjusted Plus-Minus (RAPM) uses stint-level lineup indicators to estimate player contributions to scoring margin. When low-minute player columns are removed, their stints remain in the data, but the design matrix no longer represents the complete lineup. Dummy RAPM restores this information using five indicators for the number of excluded players on each lineup side. Across 16 NBA seasons, chronological validation selects a 10-minute-per-appearance threshold and a dummy-to-player penalty ratio of 2.2. On held-out March-April games, Dummy RAPM reduces mean season game-margin RMSE from 12.897 to 12.856 and achieves lower RMSE in 13 of 16 seasons. The average reduction is 0.042 points, or 0.30%. Although the improvement in game-level predictive accuracy is small, it is consistent: RAPM performs better when it records how many excluded players are on each side.

</details>

*Paromita Banerjee*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文对美国各州COVID-19传播动态进行了回顾性分析，采用改进的SEIARD仓室模型，将时变传播率建模为人口流动性与剩余时间衰减项的函数，并通过贝叶斯方法整合多源数据量化不确定性，最后通过聚类分析识别具有相似流行病学特征的州群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在捕捉疫情期间公众行为和政策干预的时序变化，量化各州流行病学参数的时空异质性，为医疗资源分配和公共卫生干预评估提供数据驱动的决策支持。

Result: 模型成功揭示了各州疫情轨迹的差异，聚类分析发现了具有相似流行病学特征的州群，表明各州在传播强度、再生动态和死亡负担方面存在显著的地区异质性。

Conclusion: 该回顾性建模框架能为未来大流行期间的医疗资源优化配置和公共卫生干预效果评估提供支持，同时揭示了美国各州之间显著的区域异质性，强调了州级层面精细化分析和干预的重要性。

Abstract: We conduct a retrospective analysis of COVID-19 transmission dynamics across U.S. states using a modified population-based Susceptible-Exposed-Infectious-Asymptomatic-Recovered-Deceased (SEIARD) compartmental model. The proposed framework introduces time-varying transmission, reporting, and mortality rates to capture temporal variations in public behavior and policy interventions during the pandemic. In particular, the transmission rate is modeled as a function of population mobility (derived from Google Mobility Reports), with a residual time-decay term capturing the net effect of unobserved factors such as behavioral adaptation and control measures, while reporting is linked to nationwide testing strategies. We employ a Bayesian approach to integrate multiple data sources and quantify uncertainties in model parameters. The model explicitly distinguishes between symptomatic and asymptomatic infectious individuals and links the latent epidemic states to observable quantities, including reported cases and deaths, through a dynamic reporting function. This retrospective modeling framework provides insights into state-level epidemic trajectories and supports data-driven decision-making for optimal allocation of healthcare resources and evaluation of public health interventions during future pandemics. We further apply a clustering analysis to the posterior parameter estimates to identify groups of U.S. states exhibiting similar epidemiological characteristics, revealing substantial regional heterogeneity in transmission intensity, reproduction dynamics, and mortality burden.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球孢子菌病是一种地方性真菌肺部感染，Thompson等人2017年的研究发现许多球孢子菌分离株对氟康唑的MIC值较高，但对其他三唑类药物MIC值较低，提示可能存在交叉药物耐药模式。理解药物之间MIC值的联合分布结构对于优化临床治疗决策和耐药性监测至关重要。

Result: 在选定的重构数据上，强制四簇划分一致识别出一个上卡泊芬净MIC尾部。在评估的相关性情景中，gap统计量在96%至100%的嵌套重构中倾向于单一簇，表明重构的多变量数据不支持更广泛的多样簇结构。模拟研究中，多类调整兰德指数随方法和流行率从中等至高不等，而上严重度类别的二元分割一致性在1%流行率时接近零，尽管召回率仍接近1.0。

Conclusion: 聚类结论依赖于假定的跨药物依赖结构和目标类别中的实际观测数量。当目标类别在低流行率下观测数极少时，即使召回率较高，二元分割一致性也可能接近零，提示在低流行率场景下聚类分析作为分类工具存在局限性。

Abstract: Coccidioidomycosis is a fungal lung infection endemic to parts of the Pacific Northwest and southwestern United States, Mexico, Central America, and South America. A 2017 study by Thompson et al. reported that many analyzed Coccidioides isolates had elevated minimum inhibitory concentration values for fluconazole but comparatively low minimum inhibitory concentration values for other triazole drugs. We constructed 172 synthetic joint minimum inhibitory concentration profiles from the published drug-specific marginal frequency tables. Values sampled from each marginal distribution were paired across drugs using a Gaussian copula, and potential cross-drug patterns were examined using k-means clustering, hierarchical clustering, and Gaussian mixture models. On the selected reconstruction, a forced four-cluster partition consistently identified an upper caspofungin minimum-inhibitory-concentration tail. Across the evaluated dependence scenarios, the gap statistic favored a single cluster in 96% to 100% of nested reconstructions, providing no evidence that the reconstructed multivariate data supported a broader multicluster structure. A separate simulation study examined recovery of an upper-severity-score category defined from prespecified quantiles of a composite minimum-inhibitory-concentration score. The study included 4,000 replications across 20 prevalence and measurement-noise conditions. The multiclass adjusted Rand index ranged from moderate to high depending on the method and prevalence, whereas binary partition agreement for the upper-severity-score category approached zero at 1% prevalence even though recall remained near 1.0. Clustering conclusions therefore depended on the assumed cross-drug dependence structure and on the realized number of observations in the target category.

</details>


### [62] [Integrating Temporal Disaggregation and Distributed Lag Nonlinear Models for Bayesian Spatio-Temporal Disease Mapping with High-Resolution Environmental Exposures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20046)
*Alejandro Rozo Posada,Maxime Fajgenblat,Christel Faes,James Colborn,Emanuele Giorgi,Baltazar Candrinho,Thomas Neyens*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯时空框架，通过潜在日尺度疾病过程与月观测疟疾计数的时域分解（temporal disaggregation），解决环境暴露精细分辨率与健康结局粗时间尺度之间的时间错配问题，并整合分布式滞后非线性模型（DLNM）刻画气候的延迟与非线效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流行病学分析通常将精细的环境数据聚合到与健康结局相同的时间尺度，这会模糊环境暴露与疾病之间的延迟和非线性关系。马干达等疟疾高负担地区的监测数据以月度统计，而环境暴露（如温度、降水）可在日尺度获取，两者时间分辨率不匹配制约了因果推断的准确性。

Method: 构建统一的贝叶斯分层模型，包含三个核心组件：(1) 潜在日尺度疾病过程通过时间分解与观测的月度疟疾计数关联；(2) 分布式滞后非线性模型建模气候因素（温度、降水、相对湿度、植被）的延迟及非线性效应；(3) 时空随机效应与干预协变量纳入同一框架。数据来自莫桑比克161个地区2017-2024年的疟疾监测。

Result: 相较于传统月度聚合模型，该框架在广州预测准确性和不确定性量化方面表现更优。估计结果揭示了气候变异与疟疾发病率之间的非线性关系：存在最适温度区间，植被正异常增加传播风险，降水呈现非线性效应。

Conclusion: 该框架通过避免环境暴露的时间聚合，为从常规监测数据中探究环境延迟效应提供了灵活工具，并可推广至其他具有时间分辨率错配的环境敏感性疾病的流行病学研究。

Abstract: Environmental conditions are major drivers of malaria transmission, but epidemiological analyses are often constrained by temporal misalignment between health outcomes reported at coarse time scales and environmental exposures available at finer resolutions. Conventional approaches aggregate environmental data to match health outcomes, potentially obscuring delayed and nonlinear relationships. We propose a Bayesian spatio-temporal framework that addresses this limitation through a latent daily disease process linked to observed monthly malaria counts by temporal disaggregation. The framework integrates distributed lag nonlinear models for climatic effects, spatio-temporal random effects, and intervention covariates within a unified hierarchical model.
  The methodology was applied to malaria surveillance data from 161 districts in Mozambique between 2017 and 2024, integrating temperature, precipitation, relative humidity, vegetation, elevation, and malaria interventions. Compared with a conventional monthly model, the proposed framework improved predictive accuracy and uncertainty quantification while exploiting the temporal resolution of environmental data. Estimated relationships showed nonlinear associations between climatic variability and malaria incidence, including an optimal temperature range, increasing risk with positive vegetation anomalies, and nonlinear precipitation effects.
  By avoiding temporal aggregation of environmental exposures, the framework provides a flexible approach for investigating delayed environmental effects from routine surveillance data and can be extended to other environmentally sensitive diseases with mismatched temporal resolutions.

</details>

Abstract: Let $A$ be a ring and let $\bf T$ be an $n$-term big tilting complex over $A$, represented by a bounded complex $\cpx{P}$ of projective $A$-modules. We construct an extension-closed exact subcategory $\mathscr E$ of $B\Modcat$ and prove that the derived category $\D{B}$ admits a recollement by $\D{\mathscr E}$ and $\D{A}$. The proof passes through a dg double-centralizer description of a projective model of $\cpx{T}$ and an exact realisation theorem identifying $\D{\mathscr E}$ with the kernel of the derived tensor functor. We further show that $\mathscr E$ is $d$-symmetric for every $d$ not smaller than the amplitude of a perfect right $B$-model of $\cpx{T}$. Moreover, $\mathscr E$ is abelian if and only if the kernel is stable under the standard $t$-structure, equivalently, the recollement is induced by a homological ring epimorphism. In right $B$-amplitude at most one, the kernel is therefore abelian, and our construction specializes to the recollement arising from universal localisation in the two-term case. Thus the classical two-term picture extends to arbitrary finite-term big tilting complexes, with exact categories replacing abelian kernels in higher amplitude. Finally, we construct genuinely $n$-term non-compact big tilting complexes for every $n\geq2$, including an explicit three-term example whose left-hand term is determined by an algebraic Calkin-type quotient.

Abstract: Let $\text{F}$ be a non-archimedean local field of characteristic zero with residual characteristic $p$. The computation on the automorphic side requires neither the tameness condition nor the characteristic zero condition.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交织距离理论需要将卷积算子迭代合成作为流（flow）来运作，即LCa∘LCb≃LCa+b。作者希望系统理解在何种偏序集上这一合成律成立，以及失败的机制是什么，从而筛选出可以定义扩展交织伪度量的范畴。

Result: 最终性足以保证流成立；在Φ的最终性缺陷本质处，流成立等价于每一长度二区间恰有一个内元。但该条件在所有长度二区间的分支处失败，因此在每个维度≥2的有限正则胞腔复形的面偏序集上失败。在温和偏序集上流成立，扩展交织伪度量在饱和情形下不取超过P长度的有限值，且有限当且仅当导出共极限一致。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已在射电、光学/UV、X射线和GeV波段观测到GRB余晖中早期光度与后期平均衰减速率之间存在固有相关性，表明更亮的余晖倾向于有更高的平均衰减速率。本文旨在利用更大的样本（427个SGRB和LGRB）对这一相关性进行定量验证，并探讨其物理起源。

Result: 全样本中L_X,200s与α_X,>200s的Spearman秩相关系数为0.54±0.04，显著性≥3σ，线性回归斜率0.18±0.02。LGRB中相关性显著（≥3σ），但SGRB中不显著。在102个光变曲线采样良好的LGRB子样本中，相关性强度显著增强至R_sp=0.80±0.04，斜率与全样本保持一致。

Abstract: The intrinsic luminosity-decay correlation in gamma-ray burst (GRB) afterglows, between the early-time luminosity and the average rate of decay past this time, has previously been observed in the radio, optical/UV, X-ray and GeV wavebands and quantitatively shows that more luminous afterglows tend to have higher average rates of decay. We have compiled an updated sample of 427 X-ray afterglows with measured redshifts, observed with Swift/XRT over 20 years. For each GRB, we measure the luminosity at 200 seconds in the rest frame, $L_{\mathrm{X,200s}}$, and the average rate of decay from this time, $α_{X,>200s}$. We find these parameters are correlated with a Spearman's rank coefficient ($R_\mathrm{sp}$) of $0.54\pm0.04$ at a significance of $\geq3σ$ and a linear regression slope of $0.18\pm0.02$. We separate our sample into subsamples, including 395 long GRBs (LGRBs) and 32 short GRBs (SGRBs) and find evidence of the $L_{\mathrm{X,200s}}$-$α_{X,>200s}$ correlation at a significance of $\geq3σ$ in LGRBs but not in SGRBs, consistent with previous studies. In a subsample of 102 LGRBs with well-sampled light curves and late end times, we find that scatter in the correlation is significantly reduced and the strength increases to $R_\mathrm{sp}=0.80\pm0.04$ whilst the slope remains consistent with the full sample. We discuss our results and, briefly, their potential implications on constraining the cause of the correlation. Possible causes include geometric effects due to the angle between the observer and the jet-axis, or some mechanism that regulates the rate at which energy is released by the GRB central engine.

### [74] [Modeling Relativistic Tidal Disruptions of MESA Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19402)
*Liam M. Wang,Giovanni Maria Tomaselli,Zihan Zhou*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文为潮汐瓦解事件（TDEs）的牛顿模型添加了相对论修正，包括赤道Kerr测地线、相对论潮汐场和相对论回落时间，发现恒星会更早被瓦解、回落时间更长，但黑洞自旋对回落时间影响很小（除非轨道极近）。模型计算廉价且代码开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统TDE研究依赖计算开销巨大的流体动力学模拟，而现有的牛顿解析模型（基于MESA恒星的恒星扰动理论）忽略了广义相对论效应，导致瓦解时机和回落时间计算不够准确。作者希望在不牺牲计算效率的前提下提高模型精度。

Method: 在已有牛顿TDE模型基础上，加入三类相对论修正：(1) 使用赤道Kerr测地线描述恒星轨道；(2) 采用相对论潮汐场计算恒星形变；(3) 使用相对论回落时间公式。将该结果与纯牛顿情形对比分析。

Result: 相对论修正导致恒星在轨道中更早被瓦解（累积形变时间更短），且瓦解时与黑洞距离更远使得回落时间延长。黑洞自旋对回落时间影响可忽略，除非轨道近星点极近。模型在精度提升的同时保持计算开销低廉。

Conclusion: 该相对论修正模型比牛顿模型能更准确地计算TDE的回落率，同时保持计算成本优势，代码已在GitHub公开，为TDE观测与理论对比提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Tidal disruption events (TDEs) occur when a star passes so close to a black hole that its self-gravity is overcome by the external tidal field. As the star passes, it initially deforms, then is ripped apart, and some of its material eventually falls back on bound orbits, forming an accretion disk around the black hole. A Newtonian model of TDEs, based on stellar perturbation theory of MESA stars, was recently introduced as an alternative to computationally intensive hydrodynamical simulations. In this work, we add relativistic corrections to the model, incorporating equatorial Kerr geodesics, relativistic tidal fields, and relativistic fallback times. Compared to the Newtonian case, we find that stars are disrupted earlier in their orbit, which gives them less time to accumulate physical deformations. Additionally, we find that the increased distance from the black hole at the time of disruption makes the fallback time longer. However, the black hole spin has a negligible impact on fallback time, except for orbits with exceptionally close pericenter. Our results allow for a more accurate calculation of fallback rates than the Newtonian model, while also remaining computationally cheap. The code is available on GitHub.

</details>


### [75] [Outflows in steep density gradients: diversity of behavior and implications for tidal disruption events and luminous fast blue optical transients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19512)
*Benjamin Amend,Michael Camilo,Eric R. Coughlin,Anna Y. Q. Ho,Jonathan Zrake*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在绝热膨胀阶段，中心引擎驱动的风注入周围气体时产生的双激波（正向激波与反向激波）动力学。论文发现，风与周围介质的密度比 $f$ 决定了不同的动力学极限：当 $f \ll 1$ 时，反向激波位于接触间断内部，激波风区域呈近似等压结构驱动壳层；当 $f \gg 1$ 时，反向激波靠近接触面，膨胀由自由膨胀风与扫过周围气体的冲压压力相互作用主导。通过解析标度关系和一维激波捕捉流体动力学模拟，论文揭示在 $n>2$ 的陡峭环境密度轮廓（$\rho_a \propto r^{-n}$）中，初始欠密外向流会经历加速阶段（$R_s \propto t^{3/(5-n)}$），随后因 $f$ 随半径增大而过渡到恒速膨胀；而初始过密外向流则始终近似匀速coasting。研究结果适用于潮汐瓦解事件外向流和明亮蓝色快速光学暂现源的建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解功率爆炸期间中心引擎持续注入能量所产生的双激波结构在不同环境密度轮廓下的动力学演化，特别是确定正向激波何时加速、何时匀速，以更好地解释观测到的天体物理瞬现源（如潮汐瓦解事件和明亮蓝色快速光学暂现源）的外向流行为。

Method: 采用解析标度关系推导在不同密度极限（$f \ll 1$ 和 $f \gg 1$）下的激波演化规律，并结合一维激波捕获流体动力学数值模拟，在环境密度轮廓 $\rho_a \propto r^{-n}$（$2 \leq n \leq 3$）中检验并验证解析结果，包括追踪正向激波半径随时间演化、激波结构加速或匀速过渡的临界条件。

Result: （1）对于 $n > 2$，初始欠密外向流（$f_0 \ll 1$）产生加速的正向激波，半径演化遵循 $R_s \propto t^{3/(5-n)}$；（2）由于风密度 $\rho_w \propto r^{-2}$，$f$ 随半径增大，当 $f \sim$ 几时（$t_{\rm dec} \propto f_0^{1/(2-n)}$），激波过渡到恒速膨胀；（3）初始过密外向流（$f_0 \gg 1$）不经历显著加速阶段，在整个绝热演化过程中近似匀速coasting。

Conclusion: 该工作系统刻画了风注入驱动的双激波系统在陡峭环境密度轮廓下的两种动力学极限及其之间的转变，为解释潮汐瓦解事件和明亮蓝色快速光学暂现源等观测现象中外向流的加速与匀速行为提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Powerful explosions may undergo sustained energy injection as a central engine launches a wind into the surrounding gas, generating a forward and a reverse shock separated by a contact discontinuity. During the adiabatic phase, the dynamics depend strongly on the wind-to-ambient density ratio $f \equiv ρ_{\rm w} / ρ_{\rm a}$. For $f << 1$, the reverse shock lies well inside the contact discontinuity, and the mechanical energy deposited by the wind is retained in a radially extended, approximately isobaric shocked-wind region whose pressure drives the swept-up ambient shell. For $f \gg 1$, the reverse shock remains close to the contact, and the expansion is governed by the ram-pressure interaction between the freely expanding wind and the swept-up ambient gas. We use analytic scalings and one-dimensional shock-capturing hydrodynamic simulations to determine how outflows in these two limits evolve in ambient density profiles $ρ_{\rm a} \propto r^{-n}$, where $2 \leq n \leq 3$, and whether their shock structures accelerate or coast at constant velocity. For $n > 2$, initially underdense outflows produce accelerating forward shocks whose radii evolve as $R_{\rm s} \propto t^{3/(5-n)}$. Because $ρ_{\rm w} \propto r^{-2}$, f increases with radius, causing the reverse-shocked wind region to contract relative to the contact position as the forward shock transitions toward constant-velocity expansion. This occurs when $f \sim$ a few at $t_{\rm dec} \propto f_0^{1/(2-n)}$, where $f_0$ is the initial wind-to-ambient density ratio. By contrast, outflows initialized with $f_0 \gg 1$ do not develop an extended accelerating phase and remain approximately coasting throughout their adiabatic evolution. We discuss applications to tidal disruption event outflows and luminous fast blue optical transients, whose environments are often inferred to have steep density profiles with $n > 2$.

</details>


### [76] [Discovery of Three Glitches in the previously quiet pulsar PSR J1637$-$4642](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19555)
*Zhaoyi Wang,Chuwen Zheng,Yuhong Zhuang,Hanyu Zhang,Peng Liu,Zhonghao Tu,Ang Li*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报告了年轻脉冲星PSR J1637−4642的3次旋转glitch事件的发现与分析，基于2009至2024年间Murriyang射电望远镜的计时观测数据。最强的一次glitch（MJD 58352）呈现出约100天的指数恢复和永久自转减慢率增加，通过涡旋爬移框架的贝叶斯建模得出超流转动惯量分数约0.0187，与内部壳层超流一致，支持标准超流glitch范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探索此前未显示glitch活动的"安静"年轻脉冲星是否仍可能经历显著的glitch事件，并通过详细的post-glitch建模检验标准超流glitch理论在低活动性脉冲星中的适用性。

Result: 探测到3次glitch：最强的在MJD 58352，频率相对变化约2.7×10⁻⁶；另外两次较小（约2.2×10⁻⁹和2.8×10⁻⁸）。仅第一次glitch出现可检测的指数恢复，衰减时标约100天，恢复分数约0.015，并伴随自转减慢率幅度的永久增加。Bayesian建模得出超流转动惯量分数约0.0187。

Conclusion: 研究结果与标准超流glitch范式一致，表明即使历史上没有glitch活动的"安静"脉冲星也可能产生显著的glitch事件，其超流转动惯量分数与内部壳层超流理论预期相符，丰富了我们对脉冲星glitch多样性和超流特性分布的理解。

Abstract: We present the discovery and analysis of three rotational glitches in the young pulsar PSR J1637$-$4642. The timing observations span from 19 February 2009 to 6 October 2024 (MJD 54881$-$60589) from the Murriyang radio telescope of the Parkes Observatory. The first and strongest glitch occurred around MJD 58352 with a fractional frequency change of $Δν/ν\sim 2.7 \times 10^{-6}$, while two additional smaller glitches were detected at MJD 59443 and MJD 60445 with fractional changes of $2.2 \times 10^{-9}$ and $2.8 \times 10^{-8}$, respectively. Prior to this, the pulsar had shown no glitch activity since its discovery in the Parkes Multibeam survey. Only the first glitch exhibits detectable exponential recovery, with a decay timescale of $\sim$100 days and a small recovery fraction $\approx 0.015$, accompanied by a permanent increase in the magnitude of the spin-down rate. Modeling the post-glitch evolution of $\dotν$ within the vortex-creep framework using Bayesian inference gives a superfluid moment-of-inertia fraction $\approx 0.0187$, consistent with the inner-crust superfluid. These results reinforce the standard superfluid glitch paradigm and demonstrate that even ``quiet'' pulsars can still host substantial glitch activity.

</details>


### [77] [First Observational Evidence for QPO-like Coevolution between Characteristic Damping Timescales and X-ray Time Lags among AGNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19610)
*Haoyang Zhang,Li Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了窄线seyfert 1星系RE J1034+396的多历元XMM-Newton观测数据，通过阻尼随机游走过程分别建模软波段（0.3-1 keV）和硬波段（1-4 keV）光变曲线，获得了17次观测的可靠特征阻尼时标，发现硬波段特征阻尼时标与X射线时延呈现出与先前报道的QPO频率-时延环路高度相似的逆时针闭合环路，为内吸积流/冕的循环演化同时影响QPO和随机变异的物理图景提供了首个观测支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明RE J1034+396中的准周期振荡（QPO）频率与X射线时延之间存在共同演化关系，但随机变异是否参与相同的结构演化过程仍不清楚。本研究的核心动机在于探究随机变异（以特征阻尼时标为代表）是否与QPO和时延共享同一套内吸积流结构演化图景。

Method: 采用阻尼随机游走（damped random walk）过程分别对0.3-1 keV软波段和1-4 keV硬波段的光变曲线进行建模，从17次观测中提取可靠的特征阻尼时标（CDT），并将其与X射线时延相结合，分析不同波段CDT与时延之间的参数空间轨迹关系。

Conclusion: 观测到的环路可能代表了内吸积流/冕共同循环演化的不同投影，三种计时观测量（QPO频率、时延、特征阻尼时标）分别以不同时标响应内吸积流/冕的动力学、随机和辐射特性。这为活动星系核中"内热流/冕的空间尺度变化同时影响QPO与随机变异"的物理图像提供了首次观测支持。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) and stochastic variability provide complementary probes of the inner accretion flow around supermassive black holes in active galactic nuclei (AGNs). Previous multi-epoch studies of narrow-line Seyfert 1 galaxy RE~J1034+396 revealed a coevolution between the QPO frequency and the X-ray time lag, but whether the stochastic variability participates in the same structural evolution has remained unclear. We analyze the multi-epoch XMM-Newton observations of RE~J1034+396 and model the soft (0.3--1~keV) and hard (1--4~keV) light curves separately using a damped random walk process. We obtain reliable characteristic damping timescales (CDTs) for 17 observations, with the soft-band CDT consistently longer than its hard-band counterpart. When combined with the X-ray time lag, the hard-band CDT traces a counterclockwise closed loop that closely resembles the previously reported QPO-frequency--time-lag loop, whereas the soft-band CDT exhibits more complex trajectory. Under plausible dynamical, thermal, and viscous interpretations, the hard-band CDT is associated with characteristic scales in the inner hot accretion flow/corona. The observed loops may represent different projections of a common cyclic evolution of the inner accretion flow/corona, as the three timing observables may respond to its dynamical, stochastic, and radiative properties on different timescales. These results provide the first observational support among AGNs for the physical picture in which changes in the spatial extent of the hot inner flow/corona simultaneously affect QPO and stochastic variability.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: FRB 20240114A呈现多尺度记忆框架：短时标上随机行为、长时标上月级非平稳性，为自组织临界性提供证据，为爆发模型提供基准，并强调长期高频监测对捕捉时域复杂性的必要性。

Abstract: We present a statistical analysis of FRB~20240114A, a hyperactive repeating fast radio burst, based on 11,553 bursts detected by FAST over 214 days. Our main findings are fourfold. (1) On the most active day (MJD~60381, 3,197 bursts in 4.38 hr), event-rate coherence analysis reveals persistent correlated activity extending up to 3600~s, the longest reported for any repeating FRB, showing memory persists even in intense bursting epochs. (2) The waiting-time distribution on this day is well described by three exponentials, whereas the full 214-day sample develops a threshold power-law tail, indicating burst statistics depend on the observational baseline, with long-range correlations emerging only over longer timescales, a hallmark of self-organized criticality. (3) Rescaled range (R/S) analysis of waiting times reveals a broken power law, with Hurst exponents $H_1=0.63\pm0.02$ (short-lag weak memory) and $H_2=1.04\pm0.02$ (long-lag non-stationary drift). The break corresponds to $\sim$1 hour, consistent with the 3600~s coherence limit. R/S analysis of energies similarly exhibits a break ($H_1=0.60\pm0.01$, $H_2=1.10\pm0.05$) at a different lag, reinforcing that non-stationarity affects both temporal and energetic properties. (4) Energy distributions exhibit waiting-time-dependent slopes that are consistent with the full and daily samples, and the high-energy cutoff remains constant across waiting-time groups, suggesting that the maximum energy scale is an intrinsic source property. Together, these results establish a multi-scale memory framework: the source behaves stochastically on short timescales but exhibits systemic non-stationarity over months, providing benchmarks for burst models and highlighting the need for long-term, high-cadence monitoring to capture temporal complexity.

</details>

Method: 提出双通道模型：(1) X射线通道——sBH周期性穿越带大尺度磁场的核吸积盘，引力聚焦和动力学阻力产生热光学厚抛射物，通过膨胀和光子扩散产生软X射线QPE；(2) UV通道——sBH运动压缩弯曲背景磁场，触发盘内磁重联，耗散能量经光子扩散后以延迟UV辐射形式释放。模型参数基于Bondi尺度进行校准。

Result: 模型在标准参数下产生X射线时长~10^3 s、光度~10^42 erg s^-1；低轨道倾角可将X射线时长延长至Ansky观测的日尺度。UV通道产生的热功率与Ansky观测的可变UV亮度相当。不利磁场或扩散时间超过QPE复发周期时，UV信号会被削弱或模糊，解释了其他QPE源缺乏UV对应体。

Conclusion: 该双通道模型能够统一解释QPE的软X射线发射及其在Ansky中观测到的延迟UV对应体，同时其参数依赖性可为不同QPE源是否显示UV对应体提供解释。模型预测可为未来观测验证提供具体可检验的物理机制。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic eruptions (QPEs) are recurrent soft X-ray flares from galactic nuclei, but their origin remains uncertain. The delayed ultraviolet (UV) counterpart detected in Ansky provides a new constraint on viable models. We present a two-channel model in which a satellite black hole (sBH) repeatedly crosses a nuclear accretion disk threaded by a large-scale magnetic field. Gravitational focusing and dynamical drag generate hot, optically thick ejecta whose expansion and photon diffusion power the soft X-ray QPE. For fiducial Bondi-scale parameters, the model yields a characteristic X-ray duration of $\sim10^3\ \mathrm{s}$ and luminosity of $\sim10^{42}\ \mathrm{erg\,s^{-1}}$; at lower orbital inclinations, the duration extends to the day-long scale observed in Ansky. Simultaneously, the sBH motion compresses and bends the background magnetic field, triggering in-disk reconnection. The dissipated energy then emerges after photon diffusion as a broader, delayed UV response. The resulting thermal power is comparable to the variable UV luminosity of Ansky. Unfavorable magnetic fields or diffusion times longer than the QPE recurrence period can weaken or smear out the UV signal, potentially explaining the lack of clear UV counterparts in other QPE sources.

</details>

Abstract: We present new multi-frequency VLBA observations of \object{J0011+3443} (TXS\,0008+344, $z=0.89$) at 2.3, 4.9, 8.5, and, for the first time, 23.6\,GHz. The source consists of two compact components A and B at a projected separation of $314\pm2$\,pc, plus a third feature C detected at 23.6\,GHz at $0.6$\,mas from A. Archival low-resolution radio measurements confirm an integrated gigahertz-peaked spectrum, with an observed-frame peak frequency of $ν_\mathrm{peak}=0.73\pm0.08$\,GHz and a peak flux density of $S_\mathrm{peak}=879\pm125$\,mJy. Comparison with nearly frequency-matched low-resolution measurements shows that the VLBA recovers $0.85\pm0.11$ of the 4.85\,GHz flux density and $0.50\pm0.06$ of the 8.46\,GHz flux density. The lower recovered fraction at 8.5\,GHz suggests that low-surface-brightness emission is resolved out or falls below the VLBA surface-brightness sensitivity. We therefore interpret the VLBA component spectra as spectra of the compact recovered emission only. The 23.6\,GHz morphology, the absence of a detected flat-spectrum core, the similar compact spectra of A and B, and the steep integrated GHz spectrum favor an interpretation of \object{J0011+3443} as a GPS-class compact symmetric object, possibly in a short-lived or relic phase, although a dual-AGN origin cannot be excluded without multi-epoch astrometry.

*Xiao-Fei Dong,Ik Siong Heng,Gavin P. Lamb,Chris Messenger,Nikhil Sarin,Benjamin Rayson,Nial R. Tanvir,Jessica Irwin,Thomas Wallace,Skye Rosetti,Bo Milvang-Jensen,Andrew J. Levan,Jens Hjorth*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 千新星是探测双中子星并合的关键电磁窗口，但迄今为止仅有少数候选体被探测到（AT2017gfo是唯一与引力波事件GW170817关联的）。需要一种能够直接从引力波后验样本预测千新星光变曲线与光谱的概率框架，以更充分利用未来的引力波观测数据。

Method: 使用条件归一化流（conditional normalising flow）学习静止系光谱分布，条件是源参考系分量质量、潮汐形变率、观测角和并合后时间。训练过程中对其他千新星模型参数（如喷出物不透明度）进行边际化，将其效应作为预测不确定性传播到预测光谱中。

Result: 自洽测试中，流模型重现中位光变曲线的残差通常低于~0.1星等，预测中心68%区间宽度比在0.4-8.0天范围内主要集中在0.8-1.4之间。与物理上不同的千新星模型比较显示预测超越了训练模型的可信息性。应用于GW170817/AT2017gfo时，结合新重新处理的VISTA望远镜Y、J、Ks波段测光数据，预测区间能广泛涵盖观测结果。

Conclusion: Genova提供了一个从引力波后验样本到千新星电磁预测的端到端概率框架，能够捕获引力波后验不确定性以及边际化千新星模型参数带来的变化，对未来双中子星并合事件的多信使观测具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Kilonovae provide a key electromagnetic window into binary neutron star mergers, revealing the properties of the merging system while probing r-process nucleosynthesis of the Universe. However, only a few kilonova candidates have been detected to date, with AT2017gfo being the only one associated with a gravitational wave event, GW170817. In this work, we present \textsc{Genova}, a probabilistic framework for predicting kilonova spectra and light curves directly from gravitational wave posterior samples of binary neutron star mergers. This method uses a conditional normalising flow to learn the distribution of rest-frame spectra conditioned on the source-frame component masses, tidal deformabilities, viewing angle and time since merger. Other kilonova model parameters, such as ejecta opacities, are marginalised over during training, so that their effects are propagated into the predicted spectra as predictive uncertainty. In the self-consistency test, the flow model reproduces the median light curves with residuals typically below $\sim 0.1$ mag, and the ratio of the predicted central 68\% interval widths remains predominantly between $0.8$ and $1.4$ over $\sim 0.4$--$8.0$ days. Comparisons with a physically distinct kilonova model show that the probabilistic prediction can remain informative beyond the model used for training. We apply \textsc{Genova} to GW170817/AT2017gfo using multi-band observations, including newly re-reduced $Y$-, $J$-, $K_s$-band photometry from the Visible and Infrared Survey Telescope for Astronomy, which we present in this work. The resulting predictive intervals broadly encompass the observations while capturing both gravitational wave posterior uncertainty and the variation induced by marginalised kilonova model parameters.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出一个系统无关、免优化的框架，直接构造二次投影矩阵。该框架通过证明合成降阶模型能匹配全阶系统的非线性矩并保留精确中心流形映射，确保在特定输入类别下稳态输出的渐近跟踪。

Result: 在一维阻尼波方程和输运方程这两个输运主导的基准问题上，所提框架在显著降低的维度状态下实现了高保真轨迹重构，带来了可观的在线计算节省。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了二次流形模型降阶缺乏系统理论构造方法的空白，为慢Kolmogorov n-宽度衰减的线性控制系统提供了一种高效、无需优化的降阶建模解决方案。

Abstract: Quadratic manifold-based model order reduction offers a viable pathway to circumvent the limitations of linear subspaces for linear control systems characterized by slow Kolmogorov $n$-width decay. However, a system-theoretic framework for constructing such quadratic approximations remains absent from the literature. This paper presents a system-agnostic, optimization-free framework for the direct construction of quadratic projection matrices. We prove that the synthesized reduced-order model matches the nonlinear moments of the full-order system and preserves its exact center manifold mapping, thereby ensuring asymptotic tracking of steady-state outputs under specific input classes. Numerical results on transport-dominated benchmark problems, namely, the one-dimensional damped wave and advection equations, show that the proposed framework achieves high-fidelity trajectory reconstruction within a significantly reduced-dimensional state space, yielding substantial online computational savings.

</details>


### [83] [On the Value Function of Infinite-Horizon Optimal Control of Piecewise Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19530)
*Francesco Cordiano,Kanghui He,Bart De Schutter*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究分段仿射（PWA）系统在约束无限时域最优控制（CITOC）问题中值函数的结构，指出现有工作未分析值函数是否为"正则PWA"（即紧集上具有有限仿射分段），并通过显式示例证明分段数可能为无穷，同时给出了确保值函数为正则PWA的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献（如[1]）已证明PWA系统在ℓ1或ℓ∞阶段代价下CITOC的值函数是状态的分段仿射（PWA）函数，但未深入探讨该值函数是否为"正则PWA"——即在紧集上具有有限数量的仿射片段，抑或分段数可能趋于无穷。这一问题对理论的严谨性以及学习型控制方案的可行性均具有重要意义。

Method: 作者首先构造了一个显式反例，展示CITOC中PWA值函数的仿射分段数量可以是非有限的（即无穷多）。基于该反例所揭示的结构特征，建立了易于验证的充分条件，在这些条件下可保证值函数是正则PWA函数（即紧集上仅有有限个仿射分段）。文章还通过数值例子对理论结果进行了说明与验证。

Result: 文章证明了PWA系统CITOC问题中值函数的分段数量在某些情况下可以是无穷的（通过显式示例），并提出了确保值函数为正则PWA函数的严格且易于验证的充分条件，这些结果与线性二次情形等已知结论相互补充。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了PWA系统约束最优控制中值函数结构分析的空白，澄清了"PWA"与"正则PWA"之间的区别，其理论发现为基于学习的PWA系统控制方案提供了理论基础，并通过数值算例验证了所提出条件的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the structure of the value function in constrained infinite-time optimal control (CITOC) problems of piecewise affine (PWA) systems, with $\ell_1$ or $\ell_\infty$ stage cost. Existing works, such as [1], establish that the resulting value function is PWA in the state. However, existing results do not analyze whether the value function is a proper PWA function, i.e., with a finite number of affine pieces over compact sets, or whether the number of pieces can be infinite. We show that the latter case is indeed possible by means of an explicit example, which is also instrumental in establishing rigorous and easily verifiable sufficient conditions that ensure that the resulting value function is a proper PWA function. Our theoretical findings complement well-known results, e.g., the linear-quadratic case, and serve as support for recent learning-based control schemes for PWA systems. Throughout the paper, the proposed results are illustrated by means of a numerical example.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 通过后向台阶流验证了连续时间窗收敛准则的有效性；圆柱绕流应用分析了不同流态对框架的影响；尾流恢复问题验证了其在非定常拓扑优化中的有效性；U形弯管优化进一步展示了其识别和重组复杂涡结构的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架避免了预设评估间隔的主观性，能够自动识别充分发展阶段进行一致的目标评估与伴随分析，在非定常流动拓扑优化中具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Rather than prescribing an evaluation interval a priori, the proposed framework characterizes each evolving unsteady flow using a sequence of time windows. A consecutive-window convergence criterion is introduced to automatically identify a representative time window within its fully developed stage. The objective evaluation and discrete adjoint analysis are then carried out consistently over the identified representative time window. The framework is implemented using a regularized lattice Boltzmann method-based large-eddy simulation (LBM-LES) solver together with a partial bounce-back fluid-solid model. The consecutive-window convergence criterion is first validated using the backward-facing step flow. Cylinder-flow applications are then employed to investigate the influence of different flow regimes on the proposed framework. The wake-flow recovery problem verifies its effectiveness for unsteady topology optimization, while U-bend optimization further demonstrates its capability to identify and reorganize complex vortical structures.

</details>


### [85] [High-Multiplicity Flexible Job Shops: From Exact Recurrent Fluid Attainment to Structure-Guided Finite-Horizon Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19711)
*Wenjun Zheng,Wei Qu,Weilin Cai,Jianfeng Mao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究高多样性柔性作业车间调度问题，通过流体松弛下界的有限缩放，构造了精确可行的重复离散调度方案，并开发了基于类型循环模板重放（TCTR）的算法，在676个实例上平均达到1.54%的间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高多样性柔性作业车间中，流体松弛虽然能提供可扩展的工作量下界，但其分数机分配无法为单个作业定义可行的调度方案。需要一种能够将流体最优分配转化为可行离散调度的方法，同时保证调度质量与计算效率。

Method: 通过有限缩放将流体分配转化为整数工序数，在非重叠的机器区间内放置工序，重复该布局并将跨重复的工序连接成单个作业，保持机器可行性并满足工序优先级。基于该重复结构，提出类型循环模板重放（TCTR）：使用规模不随作业副本数增长的优化模型搜索作业类型模板，并将所选模板重放到完整实例上。

Result: 在676个多样性扩展的公开柔性作业车间实例上，TCTR在所有情况下均可行，平均间隙为1.54%（相对于流体下界），优于基于作业索引的自适应大邻域搜索的5.32%和混合遗传算法的6.18%。

Conclusion: 当有限实例的作业类型构成相同时（即使有固定数量的额外作业），最优制造跨度与流体下界之间的间隙保持有界常数，相对间隙随实例增大而消失。TCTR算法具有优良的可行性与解质量，适用于高多样性柔性作业车间调度。

Abstract: High-multiplicity flexible job shops involve many copies of a small set of job types that must be scheduled on alternative machines. Fluid relaxations provide scalable workload lower bounds, but their fractional machine allocations do not define feasible schedules for individual jobs. We show that, after a suitable finite scaling, an optimal fluid allocation can be realized exactly by a feasible repeating discrete schedule. The construction scales the fluid allocation to integer operation counts, places the resulting operations in nonoverlapping machine intervals, repeats this arrangement, and links operations across repetitions into individual jobs without moving any interval, thereby enforcing job precedence while preserving machine feasibility. For growing finite instances with the same job-type composition, even with a fixed number of extra jobs, the gap between the optimal makespan and the fluid lower bound remains bounded by a constant; hence the relative gap vanishes as the instance grows. Guided by this repeated structure, we develop type-based cyclic template replay (TCTR), which searches job-type templates using an optimization model whose size does not grow with the number of job copies and replays the selected template on the full instance. On 676 multiplicity-expanded public flexible-job-shop instances, TCTR is feasible in every case and achieves a 1.54% mean gap to the fluid lower bound, compared with 5.32% for a job-indexed adaptive large-neighborhood search and 6.18% for a hybrid genetic algorithm.

</details>


### [86] [Dangling points in area-minimizing Meissner polyhedra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19747)
*Beniamin Bogosel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Meissner多面体（由单位直径的极端有限集生成）中"悬垂点"（恰好有两个直径邻居的点）对表面积最小化的影响。通过理论分析证明：删除悬垂点不会增加最小可达表面积，添加悬垂点也不会减小最小可达表面积，从而将面积最小化Meissner多面体的搜索范围缩减到不含悬垂点的生成集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Meissner多面体是一类定宽体（constant-width bodies），通常由极端有限点集生成。悬垂点作为恰好有两个直径邻居的特殊点，可能影响表面积最小化问题。作者希望明确悬垂点在面积最小化中的作用，以缩小搜索空间，降低寻找面积最小Meissner多面体的计算复杂度。

Method: 本文采用理论证明的方法。给定一个极端集，作者证明了：(1) 删除一个悬垂点后，基于该集合的Meissner多面体所能达到的最小表面积不会增加；(2) 添加一个悬垂点后，最小可达表面积不会减少。这两个结果共同表明悬垂点对面积最小化无正面贡献。

Result: 证明了删除悬垂点不会增加最小表面积，添加悬垂点不会减少最小表面积，从而将面积最小化Meissner多面体的候选搜索范围严格缩减到无悬垂点的极端生成集。

Conclusion: 悬垂点在Meissner多面体表面积最小化中不发挥必要作用，寻找面积最小化的Meissner多面体只需考虑不含悬垂点的生成集即可。

Abstract: Meissner polyhedra are constant-width bodies obtained from extremal finite sets of unit diameter. Such a generating set may contain dangling points, namely points having exactly two diametric neighbors. This article studies whether these points can play an essential role in surface-area minimization. Given an extremal set we show that the smallest surface area among the Meissner polyhedra based on it cannot increase by deleting a dangling point. Adding a dangling point cannot decrease the smallest achievable surface area. This reduces the search for area-minimizing Meissner polyhedra to generating sets without dangling points.

</details>


### [87] [A Controllability Gramain Shaping with LMI Constraints under Bures--Wasserstein Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19754)
*Koju Nishimoto,Yuki Onishi,Riku Funada,Mitsuji Sampei*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bures-Wasserstein距离的可控性Gramian整形控制器设计方法，通过最小化系统Gramian与期望Gramian之间的距离来实现对系统状态受外生输入影响的各向异性设计，并推导出LMI/SDP形式的凸优化公式以提高计算效率，兼容H₂控制的特例解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制器设计通常难以直接塑形系统可控性Gramian，即难以精确控制外生输入对系统状态的影响方向和强度。现有方法要么计算复杂、要么缺乏理论上的凸性保证。作者希望通过一种具有严格凸性的度量来形式化Gramian整形问题，同时使其能够与标准控制框架（如H₂控制）建立联系。

Method: 利用Bures-Wasserstein距离作为系统Gramian与期望Gramian之间差异的度量，将Gramian整形问题转化为在对称正定矩阵集合上的最小化问题。证明了该目标函数在SPD矩阵上的严格凸性；进而通过线性矩阵不等式（LMI）将问题转化为半定规划（SDP）求解，提升计算效率并允许加入额外LMI约束。此外，将高斯白噪声输入情形下的问题与H₂控制建立联系，阐释其为最优传输的一类特例。

Result: 数值实验展示了引导机器人各向异性可控性设计的效果，验证了通过LMI施加额外方向约束的能力；同时数值结果确认当期望Gramian趋于零时，所提方法收敛到H₂控制。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种兼具理论凸性保证与计算高效性的可控性Gramian整形框架，弥补了传统方法在灵活性和可扩展性上的不足，并揭示了与H₂控制和最优传输之间的深层理论联系。

Abstract: This paper proposes a controller design method for shaping the controllability Gramian into a desired form to design the effect from exogenous inputs to the system state. Using the Bures--Wasserstein distance, we formulate the shaping problem as the minimization of the distance between the system Gramian and a desired Gramian, and the objective function is shown to be strictly convex on the set of symmetric positive definite matrices. In addition, by deriving a semidefinite programming formulation via a linear matrix inequality (LMI), computational efficiency is improved and additional LMI constraints can be incorporated. When the exogenous input is modeled as Gaussian white noise, the proposed framework is closely related to $H_2$ control, which can be interpreted as a special case of optimal transport. Numerical examples demonstrate anisotropic controllability design for a guidance robot and verify the ability to impose additional directional constraints through LMIs. The numerical examples also confirm that the proposed method approaches $H_2$ control as the desired Gramian tends to zero.

</details>


### [88] [A Fixed-Penalty Linearized Augmented Lagrangian Method with Classical Multiplier Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19847)
*Benqi Liu,Kangkang Deng,Zichen Wang,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种非线性残差线性化增广拉格朗日方法（NR-LALM），用于平滑非凸的确定性或随机等式约束优化问题。该方法用正则化高斯-牛顿步替代昂贵的非线性原始子问题，同时保留经典的非线性约束残差乘子更新。确定性情形下O(ε⁻²)次迭代达到ε-近似KKT点，随机情形下期望O(ε⁻³)次随机梯度评估。所有理论结果在Lean 4中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增广拉格朗日方法对非线性等式约束优化有效，但其内部非线性原始子问题的求解代价高昂。需要一种方法既能保留增广拉格朗日的优势，又能避免昂贵的子问题求解，同时处理确定性和随机目标函数。

Method: 1) 将原始非线性子问题替换为正则化高斯-牛顿型步，仅需求解一个对称正定线性系统；2) 保留经典的非线性约束残差乘子更新；3) 分析线性化原始模型与非线性残差更新不匹配产生的二次约束线性化误差，证明其在局部正则性下可控；4) 对随机目标使用带安全重启的投影随机路径积分微分估计器；5) 引入最小范数二阶校正以降低约束线性化误差；6) 在Lean 4中形式化所有理论结果。

Result: 确定性NR-LALM在O(ε⁻²)次迭代和一次预估值评估内找到ε-近似KKT对；随机情形下期望O(ε⁻³)次随机梯度评估、O(ε⁻²)次约束和雅可比评估。紧凑性和Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz条件下得到确定性原始-对偶序列的有限长度收敛。二阶校正将约束线性化误差从二阶降至四阶而不改变复杂度阶。数值实验验证了预测的误差阶并在高维问题中表现优越。

Conclusion: NR-LALM通过线性化替换有效降低了增广拉格朗日方法的子问题求解成本，获得了与现有最优方法相符的复杂度阶，且乘子有界性和轨迹局部化被证明而非假设。全部理论结果经Lean 4形式化验证，确保了证明的可靠性。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian methods are effective for nonlinear equality-constrained optimization, but solving their nonlinear primal subproblems can be expensive. For smooth nonconvex problems with deterministic or stochastic objectives, we propose a nonlinear-residual linearized augmented Lagrangian method (NR-LALM) that replaces this subproblem by a regularized Gauss-Newton-type step while retaining the classical multiplier update based on the nonlinear constraint residual. The resulting step is computed from one symmetric positive-definite linear system, but the mismatch between the linearized primal model and the nonlinear-residual update produces a quadratic constraint-linearization error in the multiplier identity. We show that this error can be controlled under local regularity; multiplier boundedness and trajectory localization are derived rather than assumed. With fixed, accuracy-independent parameters, deterministic NR-LALM finds an $\varepsilon$-approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) pair in $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iterations and first-order oracle evaluations. For stochastic objectives, a projected stochastic path-integrated differential estimator with safeguarded restarts requires, in expectation, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ stochastic-gradient evaluations and $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ constraint and Jacobian evaluations. Compactness and a Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz condition further yield finite-length convergence of the deterministic primal-dual sequence. An optional minimum-norm second-order correction reduces the constraint-linearization error from second to fourth order without changing the complexity orders. All theoretical results are formalized in Lean 4. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted error orders and show favorable performance on high-dimensional deterministic and stochastic problems.

</details>


### [89] [To Control or not to Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19862)
*Odysseas Kanavetas,Camiel M. P. Koopmans,Floske M. Spieksma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带"控制休假"（control vacations）的M/M/1队列模型，替代传统的永久控制队列系统。在控制期结束后进入指数分布的休假期，期间使用固定服务率μ；下次控制期的启动需要直接支付成本。基于Kanavetas等人的马尔可夫决策过程，推导出平均期望成本可降低的充分条件，并据此构造成本降低策略，通过Tauberian定理将更新奖励过程的平均期望成本与消失折扣方法相关联，解析求解成本降低的下界，最后通过仿真验证实际成本降低效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的队列控制系统通常采用永久控制，这在实践中可能不够灵活或成本过高。本文引入"控制休假"的概念，通过间歇性而非持续性的服务率控制来降低成本，弥补了永久控制模型在实用性和经济性上的不足。

Method: 1) 建模为M/M/1队列，含两种可用服务率的临时控制期（指数分布长度）和控制休假期（固定服务率μ）；2) 利用Kanavetas等人的马尔可夫决策过程推导成本可降的充分条件；3) 利用相关过程的性质构造成本降低策略；4) 证明特定策略下过程诱导更新奖励过程，使用Tauberian定理将平均期望成本与消失折扣方法关联并解析求解成本降低的下界；5) 通过仿真研究实际成本降低效果。

Result: 推导出确保平均期望成本低于仅使用固定服务率模型的条件（充分条件），在该条件下构造了成本降低策略，解析确定了平均期望成本降低的下界，并通过仿真验证了这些策略的实际成本降低效果。

Conclusion: 控制休假机制能够在特定条件下显著降低队列系统的平均期望成本，为队列控制提供了比永久控制更经济灵活的替代方案，相关策略下过程呈现更新奖励结构，可实现可解析的成本下界保证。

Abstract: We introduce a model with control vacations instead of standard queueing control systems with permanent control. The researched model is an M/M/1 queue with temporary periods of service rate control with two available service rates. After a control period of exponentially distributed length, a control vacation is initiated during which a fixed service rate $μ$ is used. The start of the next control period needs to be scheduled directly at a certain cost. We will use the Markov Decision Process from Kanavetas et al. arXiv:2605.31573 to find a sufficient condition that ensures that the average expected cost can be reduced w.r.t. the model that only uses the fixed service rate. Under this condition we will use properties of this related process to construct a cost reducing policy. The process with control vacations under specific policies induces a renewal reward process. We use a Tauberian theorem to relate the average expected cost of this renewal reward process to a vanishing discount method and analytically determine a lower bound of the average expected cost reduction. Finally, we study the actual attained average cost reduction for these policies through simulation.

</details>


### [90] [Efficient flatness-based computation of trajectories for vehicles with many trailers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19915)
*Basile Graf,Gustave Lapierre,Philippe Müllhaupt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微分平坦性的高效算法，用于计算带n节拖车的汽车的前馈转向输入。该算法通过组合四种技术（角度中间变量、子映射递归因式分解、重标度高阶乘积/商法则、基于截断形式幂级数的复合法则），将计算复杂度从超指数级降低到O(r³)，在导数阶r=40时仍保持在1毫秒以内，而传统方法（SymPy直接符号微分和Faà di Bruno公式）分别在r=14和r=24时遭遇计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带n节拖车的汽车（n-trailer system）的微分平坦前馈转向输入计算，标准方法依赖于对平坦输出的迭代符号微分，但表达式规模随n呈超指数增长，即使借助计算机代数软件，当n约大于5时就变得难以处理，亟需更高效的计算算法。

Method: 提出一种融合四种关键技术的算法：(1) 引入角度中间变量简化表达式结构；(2) 将递归因式分解为子映射；(3) 使用重标度的高阶乘积/商法则；(4) 用基于截断形式幂级数的复合法则替代Faà di Bruno公式，从而将计算复杂度降为关于导数阶的O(r³)。

Result: 基准测试显示：SymPy直接符号微分在导数阶r=14时即超过20秒超时，Bell多项式（Faà di Bruno公式）在r=24后超过60秒超时；而所提方法在r=40时仍保持亚毫秒级计算时间，且在参考方法成功的范围内与数值结果达到机器精度一致。端到端演示了20拖车泊车机动场景的计算与动画。

Conclusion: 所提算法在保持机器精度数值结果的同时，将计算复杂度从超指数级降低到多项式级（O(r³)），突破了传统方法在拖车数量上的计算瓶颈，使得多达20节拖车的泊车规划在毫秒级内可行，为实际应用提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: We address the practical computation of feedforward steering inputs for a car with n trailers via differential flatness. The standard route (iterated symbolic differentiation of the flat output) scales prohibitively with n: even with computer-algebra software the expression sizes grow super-exponentially and become intractable beyond n approx. 5. We propose an algorithm that combines four ingredients (angular intermediate variables, factorisation of the recursion in submaps, rescaled higher-order product/quotient rules, and a composition rule based on truncated formal power series instead of Faa di Bruno's formula). We benchmark the proposed method against (i) direct symbolic differentiation in SymPy and (ii) Faa di Bruno's formula evaluated via Bell polynomials. Both reference methods hit a clear computational wall: SymPy reaches a 20 s timeout at derivative order r = 14, while the Bell-polynomial recursion exceeds a 60 s timeout beyond r = 24. The proposed approach has O(r3) arithmetic complexity in the derivative order and remains under one millisecond up to r = 40, with numerical agreement to machine precision in the regime where the reference methods succeed. As an end-to-end illustration, a 20-trailer parking manoeuvre is computed and animated.

</details>


### [91] [Dual Control: On Exploration-Exploitation in Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20073)
*Tomas J. Meijer,Anders Rantzer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文综述了线性时不变系统中参数未知时"双控制"（同时平衡探索与利用）的理论与研究方法，涵盖多臂老虎机、自整定调节器、最小遗憾率控制器和极小极大最优双控制器四个主要研究方向，指出第四类为鲁棒双控制提供了有前景的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双控制问题（探索与利用的双重目标）已被研究近一个世纪，但对线性时不变系统中参数未知时通过主动探测进行学习的相关理论和方法论缺乏系统性的梳理与整合。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，对四个主要研究方向（多臂老虎机、自整定调节器、遗憾率最小化控制器、极小极大最优双控制器）的核心思想进行回顾与比较分析。

Result: 前三类方法（多臂老虎机、自整定调节器、最小遗憾率控制器）具有悠久历史和丰富文献，第四类（极小极大最优双控制器）则展现出作为鲁棒双控制框架的广阔前景。

Conclusion: 极小极大最优双控制器是当前最有前途的研究方向，为鲁棒双控制问题提供了坚实框架。

Abstract: The term "dual control" refers to the dual objective of simultaneously balancing exploration and exploitation. Problems of this kind have been studied for nearly a century. This paper is devoted to theory and methodology relevant for optimal control of linear time-invariant systems whose parameters are initially unknown and must be learned by active probing. We review the main ideas underlying four major research directions: Multi-armed bandits, self-tuning regulators, regret rate minimizing controllers, and minimax optimal dual controllers. The first three have a long history and rich literature, whereas the fourth provides a promising framework for robust dual control.

</details>


### [92] [Nonlinear Controllability and the Propagation of Local Information: From the Kalman Family to Lie Brackets, Rotation Groups, and Reachable Subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20094)
*Philippe Mullhaupt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发展了从线性可控性到矩阵李群上非线性可控性的自包含李理论路径，重点研究旋转群SO(n)上局部代数信息如何传播为可达集陈述，涵盖可达到子群论证、Yamabe步骤证明及与Sussmann一般非线性系统局部可控性理论的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题在于局部代数信息能否传播为关于可达集的全局陈述。线性情形中矩阵指数、Cayley-Hamilton定理和李雅普诺夫族可将可控性归约为有限维线性代数；但在非线性情形中，李括号替代矩阵幂后，相关李代数可能是无限维的，有限维李对应可能失效，因此需要发展新的理论工具。

Method: 文章采用自包含的李理论路线：首先以线性可控性为模型，然后引入斜对称矩阵、指数映射、交换子、单参数子群及Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff公式作为SO(n)上的具体实验工具。随后发展右不变系统的可达到子群论证，给出Yambe步骤背后的详细证明序列，并通过可解、幂零、理想例子对上三角矩阵代数进行验证，最后与Sussmann的一般非线性系统局部可控性理论进行对比。

Result: 通过数值SO(3)仿真展示了受控旋转的几何特性，借助显式SO(4)和SO(7)交换子序列使局部控制方向的传播可视化；建立了从有限维代数条件到非线性可达集结论的系统化理论框架。

Conclusion: 文章表明，尽管非线性情形中李代数可能无限维且有限维对应可能失效，但通过李理论工具与可达到子群论证，线性可控性的代数思想仍可有效地推广到矩阵李群上的非线性可控性，同时明确指出其与一般非线性系统Sussmann局部可控性理论之间的差异与联系。

Abstract: This article develops a self-contained Lie-theoretic route from linear controllability to nonlinear controllability on matrix Lie groups. The organizing question is whether local algebraic information can be propagated into statements about reachable sets. The linear case supplies the model: the matrix exponential, Cayley-Hamilton theorem, trajectory formula and Kalman family B,AB,...,An-1B reduce controllability to finite-dimensional linear algebra. In the nonlinear setting, Lie brackets replace matrix powers, but the associated Lie algebra may be infinite dimensional and the finite-dimensional Lie correspondence can fail. Particular emphasis is placed on rotation groups. Skew-symmetric matrices, their exponentials, commutators, one-parameter subgroups, and the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula provide a concrete laboratory for seeing how infinitesimal directions propagate on SO(n). A numerical SO(3) simulation illustrates the geometry of controlled rotations, while explicit SO(4) and SO(7) commutator sequences make the propagation of a localized control direction visible. The article then develops the attainable-subgroup argument for right-invariant systems, gives a detailed proof sequence behind the Yamabe step, and works through solvable, nilpotent and ideal examples for upper-triangular matrix algebras. The finite-dimensional mechanism is finally contrasted with Sussmann's local controllability theory for general nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [93] [Worst-Case Probability Bounds for Finite-Horizon Safety under Moment Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20121)
*Renato Loureiro,Torbjørn Cunis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于矩-平方和（moment-SOS）框架的方法，用于估计动力系统在有限时间范围内进入不良区域（不安全区域）的概率上界。该方法利用初始状态的矩信息，通过松弛一个测度型程序来获得概率安全证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力系统在有限时间内进入不安全区域的概率估计是安全关键系统验证中的重要问题。实际问题中，系统初始状态往往无法精确已知，只能获得有限阶矩信息或区间信息，因此需要一种仅依赖部分信息即可进行安全概率上界估计的方法。

Method: 首先将问题形式化为一个基于测度的优化程序，然后利用矩-SOS（moment-SOS）框架对其进行松弛求解；对应的对偶问题被表述为一个泛函程序，并进一步强化为平方和（SOS）程序。该对偶形式在结构上与经典的屏障函数（barrier function）技术相似，但区别在于它提供的是概率性证书而非确定性证书。

Result: 通过多个案例验证了方法的有效性，包括一个轨道中物体运动案例，证明了所提出方法能够有效估计有限时间范围内的安全概率上界。

Conclusion: 该工作将矩-SOS框架与屏障函数思想相结合，为动力系统的概率安全验证提供了一种新的计算工具。其方法与经典屏障函数技术的结构相似性表明，概率证书可以作为一种推广的屏障函数来理解，为后续研究提供了理论和实践的出发点。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of estimating upper bounds on the probability that a dynamical system will enter an undesirable region at some point within a finite time horizon. The primary source of uncertainty lies in the system's initial state, for which only a finite set of moments is known or within a prescribed interval. To tackle this problem, we formulate a measure-based program and propose its relaxation using the moment-sum-of-squares (moment-SOS) framework. The corresponding dual problem is introduced as a functional program, which is subsequently strengthened into a sum-of-squares (SOS) program. Notably, this dual formulation bears a structural resemblance to classical barrier function techniques for certifying system safety, with the key distinction that it yields a probabilistic certificate. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through multiple case studies, including a case involving an object in orbit.

</details>


### [94] [Dynamic Portfolio Optimization under CVaR Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20179)
*Anran Hu,Silvana M. Pesenti,Xiaofei Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究连续时间动态投资组合优化问题，在投资者终端损失的CVaR约束下，利用CVaR的辅助阈值表示建立最优策略存在性与强对偶性（无需市场完备性），并提出基于对偶的嵌套二分-黄金搜索算法，该算法内部迭代退化为标准无约束随机控制问题。数值实验表明风险约束无效时恢复Merton策略，有效时最优策略呈现状态依赖的不对称再配置特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态投资组合优化中引入终端损失的条件风险价值（CVaR）约束，旨在控制尾部风险。现有研究大多假设市场完备或依赖特定对偶框架，本文拟在更一般的凸交易目标类下，不要求市场完备性即可建立最优策略存在性与对偶结果，并设计可实际计算的算法。

Method: 采用CVaR的辅助阈值表示（auxiliary-threshold representation），在一般凸交易目标下证明最优策略存在性与强对偶性。在此基础上提出嵌套的双层算法：外层对阈值和Lagrange乘子进行二分-黄金搜索，内层迭代化为标准无约束随机控制问题求解。同时证明迭代策略随迭代次数趋于无穷时收敛到最优控制。

Result: 数值实验表明：约束非有效时恢复Merton最优消费/投资策略；约束有效时最优策略呈状态依赖——不利结果后降低风险敞口，有利结果后保持甚至临近到期时增加敞口。终端CVaR约束产生跨状态的不对称再配置而非均匀降风险。非交易禀赋风险放大保守调整，价格冲击降低期望持仓与调整速度。

Conclusion: 终端CVaR约束下的动态最优策略具有状态依赖的不对称再配置特征，而非简单的均匀去风险化；所提出的对偶嵌套算法在一般凸目标与市场不完全条件下有效且收敛。

Abstract: We study continuous-time dynamic portfolio optimization under a Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) constraint on the investor's terminal loss. For a general class of convex trading objectives, we exploit the auxiliary-threshold representation of CVaR to establish the existence of an optimal strategy and strong duality without requiring market completeness. These results motivate a dual-based nested bisection--golden-search algorithm over the threshold and Lagrangian multiplier, where the inner iterations reduce to standard unconstrained stochastic control problems. We prove that the resulting strategies converge to the optimal control as the number of iterations tends to infinity. Numerical experiments recover the Merton policy when the risk constraint is nonbinding. When the constraint is binding, the optimal strategy becomes state dependent: the investor reduces risky exposure following adverse outcomes but preserves, and near maturity may increase, exposure following favorable outcomes. Thus, a terminal CVaR constraint produces an asymmetric reallocation across states rather than uniform de-risking. Nontraded endowment risk amplifies the conservative adjustment, whereas price impact lowers desired positions and adjustment speeds.

</details>


### [95] [Direct vs. Indirect Data-Driven Control: Case-study of Switching Systems Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20207)
*Alexis Vuille,Guillaume O. Berger,Raphaël M. Jungers*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对数据驱动控制中的直接法与间接法这一核心方法论问题，提出了一种基于间接方法和二次Lyapunov分析的切换线性系统稳定性分析新框架，该框架在含噪状态测量下提供了有限样本收敛率保证，并通过数值实验表明在中高噪声水平下间接法优于直接法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动控制中直接法和间接法的选择是核心方法论问题。直接法近期已获得切换线性系统数据驱动稳定性分析的有限样本保证，但间接法在此方面的保证仍然基本缺失。作者希望填补这一空白，为间接法提供同样严格的有限样本理论保证。

Method: 作者将机器学习中的泛化界、系统辨识与二次Lyapunov分析的敏感性分析相结合，构建了间接法下切换线性系统从含噪状态测量中进行稳定性分析的理论框架，并提供有限样本下的收敛率保证。同时，将现有直接数据驱动方法扩展至更一般的测量噪声场景（超越当前文献仅处理的有限噪声情形）。最后通过数值实验对比两种方法。

Result: 数值实验表明，在中高噪声水平下，间接法相比直接法提供更紧的概率保证，且对噪声和离群点具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 间接法在含噪场景下可以匹敌甚至超越直接法的理论保证，为数据驱动控制中方法论选择提供了重要理论依据和实用指导。

Abstract: A central methodological question in data-driven control is whether to adopt a direct or indirect approach. Direct methods infer a controller or certificate directly from data, while indirect methods first identify a system model and then apply model-based control techniques. Recent developments of the direct method have led to finite-sample guarantees for the data-driven stability analysis of switched linear systems under various settings. However, for the indirect method, such guarantees remain largely elusive. In this paper, we provide a novel framework for the stability analysis of switched linear systems from noisy state measurements, using the indirect approach and quadratic Lyapunov analysis. Our framework comes with finite-sample guarantees on the convergence rate of the system. For that, we combine generalization bounds from machine learning and system identification with sensitivity analysis from quadratic Lyapunov analysis. To enable comparison, we also extend existing direct data-driven methods to handle measurement noise beyond the bounded noise case currently available in the literature. Finally, we compare the two approaches through numerical experiments, revealing that under moderate-to-high noise levels the indirect approach yields tighter probabilistic guarantees as well as greater robustness to noise and outliers than the direct approach

</details>


### [96] [Directional Subdifferentials of the Value Function in Asplund Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20241)
*Weihao Mao,Jane J. Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Asplund空间中建立了值函数方向性次微分的变分分析框架，引入新的方向性条件克服无穷维空间中紧性缺失的问题，推导出值函数方向极限次微分与奇异次微分的上界估计，为无穷维层次系统的灵敏度分析提供了精细的分析基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于值函数方向性次微分的结果主要集中在有限维空间中，缺乏适用于无穷维空间（如Asplund空间）的综合变分框架。无穷维空间中集合的有界性不蕴含紧性，这给方向性次微分的推导带来了本质困难，需要新的工具和条件来解决。

Method: 本文在Asplund空间中发展了方向性非光滑分析的变分框架，建立了关键的微积分法则，并针对无穷维空间中紧性缺失的问题引入了一种新的方向性条件，在此条件下推导值函数的方向性极限次微分和奇异次微分的上界估计。

Result: 成功建立了Asplund空间中值函数方向性次微分的上界估计结果，包括方向性极限次微分和奇异次微分的上界，克服了无穷维空间中有界集非紧带来的技术障碍。

Conclusion: 本文为无穷维层次系统的灵敏度分析提供了更精细的变分分析基础，填补了方向性非光滑分析从有限维推广到无穷维空间的空白。

Abstract: Directional subdifferentials of the value function provide a quantitative measure of optimal value response to perturbations. While existing results are largely limited to finite-dimensional settings, this paper develops a comprehensive variational framework in Asplund spaces. We establish essential directional calculus rules, extending directional nonsmooth analysis to infinite dimensions. To address the lack of compactness of bounded sets in infinite-dimensional spaces, we introduce a new directional condition, under which we derive upper estimates for directional limiting and singular subdifferentials of the value function. These results provide a refined analytical foundation for sensitivity analysis in infinite-dimensional hierarchical systems.

</details>
